Phoenix, Arizona -Sirs: 

Re: FORTY-SECOND ANNUAL REPORT INSURANCE DEPARTMENT OF ARIZONA 

Pursuant to the provisions of law, I hereby transmit this, the 

Forty-Second Annual Report of the Insurance Department. 

Insurance company statistics shown in this annual report are on a calendar year basis, the latest year ending December 31, 1954. 

All statistics relating to Insurance Department receipts and expenditures are on a fiscal year basis with the latest fiscal year ending June 30, 1954. 

Respectfully submitted,

G. A. BUSHNELL, Director of Insurance

DUTIES AND FUNCTIONS PERFORMED BY THE INSURANCE DEPARTMENT 

The following is a general outline of duties and functions, among others, performed by the Insurance Department. 

Collect in excess of $1,775,000.00 in premium tax, fees and licenses.

Administer the provisions of the new Arizona Insurance Code of 1955. 

Examine financial statements and pass upon qualifications of companies applying for admission to do business in Arizona. 

Examine about 8,400 policies and related forms submitted for approval for use in Arizona.

Investigate and service about 2,500 reported policyholders complaints as to claims in dispute in addition to the many inquiries concerning underwriting and agency practices. 

Process about 18,500 agents and related licenses.

Prepare, supervise and grade about 1,600 written examinations of applicants for agents and related licenses.

Respond to the normal flow of correspondence, questionnaires and numerous requests for insurance information.

RECEIPTS OF THE INSURANCE DEPARTMENT OF ARIZONA 

Premium Tax and all other Fees and Licenses collected by the Insurance Department and remitted to the Department of State Treasurer for the Fiscal 

Year ended June 30, 1955, amounted to $1,775,502.46, an increase of $93.543.17, when compared with the total amount collected by the Insurance Department and remitted to the Department of State Treasurer for the proceeding Fiscal 

Year ended June 30, 1954.
